essential. I'll need to specifically target undoing and redoing winning moves. 
For an undo, I need to check that the score *decrements*, the round correctly 
returns to an active state, and that the archive of the match is properly 
removed. Then, when redoing, I need to verify that the score *restores*, the 
round becomes a win again, and the match is brought back from the archive.

const engine = require('./gameEngine.js');
const assert = require('assert');

let state = {
  round_id: 'test-round-1',
  board: JSON.stringify(engine.createEmptyBoard()),
  current_player: 'Red',
  status: 'active',
  winning_cells: null,
  red_wins: 0,
  yellow_wins: 0,
  draws: 0,
  applied_history: JSON.stringify([]),
  redo_history: JSON.stringify([]),
  revision: 0

state = res.newState;

assert.strictEqual(state.revision, 3);

b = JSON.parse(state.board);
assert.strictEqual(b[engine.getCellIndex(5, 1)], '');

assert.strictEqual(redoRes.valid, true);
state = redoRes.newState;
assert.strictEqual(state.revision, 4);
assert.strictEqual(state.current_player, 'Red');
b = JSON.parse(state.board);
assert.strictEqual(b[engine.getCellIndex(5, 1)], 'Yellow');

console.log('Basic gameEngine tests passed!');

$ node -e "
const engine = require('./gameEngine.js');
const assert = require('assert');

let state = {
  round_id: 'win-test-round',
  board: JSON.stringify(engine.createEmptyBoard()),
  current_player: 'Red',
  status: 'active',
  winning_cells: null,
  red_wins: 0,
  yellow_wins: 0,
  draws: 0,
  applied_history: JSON.stringify([]),
  redo_history: JSON.stringify([]),
  revision: 0
};

// Red wins horizontally on row 6 (cols 1, 2, 3, 4)
// Moves:
// R: 1 (row 6), Y: 1 (row 5)
// R: 2 (row 6), Y: 2 (row 5)
// R: 3 (row 6), Y: 3 (row 5)
// R: 4 (row 6) -> WIN!

state = engine.applyMove(state, 1).newState;
state = engine.applyMove(state, 1).newState;
state = engine.applyMove(state, 2).newState;
state = engine.applyMove(state, 2).newState;
state = engine.applyMove(state, 3).newState;
state = engine.applyMove(state, 3).newState;
let winMove = engine.applyMove(state, 4);
assert.strictEqual(winMove.valid, true);
assert.strictEqual(winMove.newState.status, 'Red wins');
assert.strictEqual(winMove.newState.red_wins, 1);
assert.ok(winMove.archiveMatch);
assert.strictEqual(winMove.archiveMatch.result, 'Red wins');
console.log('Winning move passed, archive match created:', 
winMove.archiveMatch.result);

// Now Undo winning move
let undoWin = engine.applyUndo(winMove.newState);
assert.strictEqual(undoWin.valid, true);
assert.strictEqual(undoWin.newState.status, 'active');
assert.strictEqual(undoWin.newState.red_wins, 0);
assert.strictEqual(undoWin.unarchiveRoundId, 'win-test-round');
console.log('Undo win passed, red_wins restored to 0, unarchiveRoundId 
returned');

// Now Redo winning move

assert.strictEqual(redoWin.valid, true);
assert.strictEqual(redoWin.newState.status, 'Red wins');
assert.strictEqual(redoWin.newState.red_wins, 1);
assert.ok(redoWin.archiveMatch);

Summary: Create /app/server.js with Express server and API routes

Reasoning:
**Server Design & API Endpoint Planning**

Alright, let's get down to brass tacks and design this `server.js` and all the 
associated API endpoints. First, I need to be absolutely meticulous about 
verifying the requirements.

I'll start with the server setup itself. It needs to listen on `0.0.0.0:3000` 
(or the environment variable `PORT`, if provided). The browser UI should be 
served from `/app/public` using `express.static`. A crucial same-origin health 
check endpoint (`GET /api/health`) returning `{ status: "ok" }` is a must.

Now, authentication. Here's what I'm thinking for the auth endpoints:

*   **`POST /api/auth/signin`**:  Accept an `{ email, password }` in the body.  
First, find the user by their email. If the user isn't found or the password 
doesn't match, return a 401 error: `{ error: "Invalid email or password" }`.  If
all checks pass, generate a secure token using 
`crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex')`.  Store this token in the `sessions` 
table alongside the user's ID and creation timestamp.  Fetch the user's 
`game_state` and `completed_matches` (both total count and the latest 10). 
Return a 200 response with `{ token, user: { name: user.name, email: user.email 
}, gameState, archive }`.

*   **`POST /api/auth/signout`**: Requires a valid Bearer token. This endpoint 
has to be aggressive. Revoke *that* token and *every other* active token 
associated with the *same account*. So, a `DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = 
req.user.id` is the right approach. Then a simple 200 response with `{ success: 
true }`.

*   **`GET /api/auth/me`**:  Needs a valid Bearer token.  If the session is 
valid, return the user's details and game data: `{ user: { name: req.user.name, 
email: req.user.email }, gameState, archive }`.  Invalid sessions or tokens 
return a 401: `{ error: "Unauthorized" }`.

Next up, the critical game endpoints. I need a robust `requireAuth` middleware 
here.  It will:

1.  Extract the `Bearer <token>` from the `Authorization` header.
2.  If missing or invalid, return a 401.
3.  Lookup the user in the `users` table. If not found, again, return a 401.
4.  If everything is valid, attach `req.user = user` and `req.sessionToken = 
token`.

I'll also need a helper function, `getArchiveForUser(db, userId)`, which 
calculates the archive data. It needs to:

1.  Get the total count of matches from `completed_matches`.
2.  Fetch the latest 10 matches, ordered by `completed_at` descending, using 
`LIMIT 10`.
3.  Map the results into a clean object with a `totalCount` and a `matches` 
array containing the relevant data (match ID, round ID, result, etc.). 
Importantly, parse any JSON fields like `finalBoard`, `moves`, and potentially 
`winning_cells`.

Another vital helper will be `formatGameState(dbRow)`. This needs to take the 
raw database row and format it into a clean JSON object for the frontend:

*   This will pull out the relevant data from the database (round ID, board, 
player info, status, winning cells, wins/losses/draws counts, applied and redo 
history, and the revision number) and reformat the JSON data as required.

Now, for the game endpoints themselves:

*   **`GET /api/game`**:  Fetch the `game_states` row for the user 
(`req.user.id`). Also fetch the archive. Return a 200 with both data objects: `{
gameState: formatGameState(stateRow), archive: getArchiveForUser(db, 
req.user.id) }`.

*   **Mutation Endpoints (move, undo, redo, new)**:  These are critical and need
bulletproof concurrency control.  I'm going to implement idempotency checks and 
a revision check with atomic database updates using transactions. Here's the 
plan:
    *   Each mutation will accept an `operationId` (string) in the request body 
(or header), or I can generate one. It will also accept an `expectedRevision`.
        *   **Step 1:** Check the `operations` table:

  command: "create"

  file_text: 
    const express = require('express');
    const path = require('path');
    const crypto = require('crypto');
    const { initDb, getDbPath } = require('./db.js');
    const gameEngine = require('./gameEngine.js');
    
    const app = express();
    const PORT = process.env.PORT || 3000;
    
    const db = initDb();
    
    app.use(express.json());
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'public')));
    
    // Helper: format game state for response
    function formatGameState(row) {
      if (!row) return null;
      return {
        roundId: row.round_id,
        board: JSON.parse(row.board),
        currentPlayer: row.current_player,
        status: row.status,
        winningCells: row.winning_cells ? JSON.parse(row.winning_cells) : null,
        redWins: row.red_wins,
        yellowWins: row.yellow_wins,
        draws: row.draws,
        appliedHistory: JSON.parse(row.applied_history),
        redoHistory: JSON.parse(row.redo_history),
        revision: row.revision
      };
    }
    
    // Helper: get archive for user
    function getArchiveForUser(userId) {
      const totalCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM 
completed_matches WHERE user_id = ?').get(userId).total;
      const rows = db.prepare(`
        SELECT id, match_id, round_id, result, final_board, moves, 
winning_cells, completed_at
        FROM completed_matches
        WHERE user_id = ?
        ORDER BY completed_at DESC, id DESC
        LIMIT 10
      `).all(userId);
    
      const matches = rows.map(r => {
        const moves = JSON.parse(r.moves);
        return {
          matchId: r.match_id,
          roundId: r.round_id,
          result: r.result,
          finalBoard: JSON.parse(r.final_board),
          moves: moves,
          moveCount: moves.length,
          winningCells: r.winning_cells ? JSON.parse(r.winning_cells) : null,
          completedAt: r.completed_at
        };
      });
    
      return {
        totalCount,
        matches
      };
    }
    
    // Auth middleware
    function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
      const authHeader = req.headers['authorization'];
      if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
      }
    
      const token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
      if (!token) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
      }
    
      const session = db.prepare('SELECT user_id FROM sessions WHERE token = 
?').get(token);
      if (!session) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
      }
    
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT id, email, name FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(session.user_id);
      if (!user) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Unauthorized' });
      }
    
      req.user = user;
      req.sessionToken = token;
      next();
    }
    
    // Health check
    app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
      res.json({ status: 'ok' });
    });
    
    // Auth endpoints
    app.post('/api/auth/signin', (req, res) => {
      const { email, password } = req.body || {};
      if (!email || !password) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE LOWER(email) = 
LOWER(?)').get(email.trim());
      if (!user || user.password !== password) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password' });
      }
    
      const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
    
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions (token, user_id, created_at) VALUES (?, 
?, ?)').run(token, user.id, now);
    
      const stateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE user_id = 
?').get(user.id);
      const archive = getArchiveForUser(user.id);
    
      res.json({
        token,
        user: {
          id: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          name: user.name
        },
        gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
        archive
      });
    });
    
    app.post('/api/auth/signout', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      // Revoke ALL active sessions for this account
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').run(req.user.id);
      res.json({ success: true });
    });
    
    app.get('/api/auth/me', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const stateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE user_id = 
?').get(req.user.id);
      const archive = getArchiveForUser(req.user.id);
    
      res.json({
        user: {
          id: req.user.id,
          email: req.user.email,
          name: req.user.name
        },
        gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
        archive
      });
    });
    
    // Game state read
    app.get('/api/game', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const stateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE user_id = 
?').get(req.user.id);
      const archive = getArchiveForUser(req.user.id);
    
      res.json({
        gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
        archive
      });
    });
    
    // Mutation helper
    function handleMutation(req, res, mutationFn) {
      const userId = req.user.id;
      const { expectedRevision, operationId } = req.body || {};
    
      // 1. Idempotency check
      if (operationId) {
        const cachedOp = db.prepare('SELECT status_code, response_json FROM 
operations WHERE user_id = ? AND op_id = ?').get(userId, operationId);
        if (cachedOp) {
          return 
res.status(cachedOp.status_code).json(JSON.parse(cachedOp.response_json));
        }
      }
    
      // 2. Fetch current state
      const stateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE user_id = 
?').get(userId);
      if (!stateRow) {
        return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Game state not found' });
      }
    
      // 3. Concurrency check
      if (typeof expectedRevision === 'number' && expectedRevision !== 
stateRow.revision) {
        return res.status(409).json({
          message: 'Game updated in another tab',
          gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
          archive: getArchiveForUser(userId)
        });
      }
    
      // 4. Execute mutation in transaction
      let responsePayload = null;
      let statusCode = 200;
    
      try {
        const tx = db.transaction(() => {
          const result = mutationFn(stateRow);
          if (!result.valid) {
            statusCode = 400;
            responsePayload = {
              message: result.error,
              gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
              archive: getArchiveForUser(userId)
            };
            return;
          }
    
          const { newState, archiveMatch, unarchiveRoundId } = result;
    
          // Update game state
          db.prepare(`
            UPDATE game_states
            SET round_id = ?, board = ?, current_player = ?, status = ?, 
winning_cells = ?,
                red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, applied_history = ?, 
redo_history = ?, revision = ?
            WHERE user_id = ?
          `).run(
            newState.round_id,
            newState.board,
            newState.current_player,
            newState.status,
            newState.winning_cells,
            newState.red_wins,
            newState.yellow_wins,
            newState.draws,
            newState.applied_history,
            newState.redo_history,
            newState.revision,
            userId
          );
    
          // Handle archive addition
          if (archiveMatch) {
            db.prepare(`
              INSERT INTO completed_matches (
                user_id, match_id, round_id, result, final_board, moves, 
winning_cells, completed_at
              ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            `).run(
              userId,
              archiveMatch.match_id,
              archiveMatch.round_id,
              archiveMatch.result,
              archiveMatch.final_board,
              archiveMatch.moves,
              archiveMatch.winning_cells,
              archiveMatch.completed_at
            );
          }
    
          // Handle archive removal (on undo of terminal state)
          if (unarchiveRoundId) {
            db.prepare('DELETE FROM completed_matches WHERE user_id = ? AND 
round_id = ?').run(userId, unarchiveRoundId);
          }
    
          const updatedStateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE 
user_id = ?').get(userId);
          const updatedArchive = getArchiveForUser(userId);
    
          responsePayload = {
            gameState: formatGameState(updatedStateRow),
            archive: updatedArchive
          };
    
          // Store in operations for idempotency
          if (operationId) {
            db.prepare(`
              INSERT OR REPLACE INTO operations (user_id, op_id, status_code, 
response_json, created_at)
              VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            `).run(userId, operationId, 200, JSON.stringify(responsePayload), 
new Date().toISOString());
          }
        });
    
        tx();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Mutation transaction error:', err);
        return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
      }
    
      return res.status(statusCode).json(responsePayload);
    }
    
    // Move mutation
    app.post('/api/game/move', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      const column = req.body.column;
      handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
        return gameEngine.applyMove(stateRow, column);
      });
    });
    
    // Undo mutation
    app.post('/api/game/undo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
        return gameEngine.applyUndo(stateRow);
      });
    });
    
    // Redo mutation
    app.post('/api/game/redo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
        return gameEngine.applyRedo(stateRow);
      });
    });
    
    // New game mutation
    app.post('/api/game/new', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
      handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
        return gameEngine.applyNewGame(stateRow);
      });
    });
    
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });
    
    // Start server
    if (require.main === module) {
      app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
        console.log(`DropLine server listening on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
        console.log(`SQLite path: ${getDbPath()}`);
      });
    }
    
    module.exports = app;

+176|    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Game state not found' });
+177|  }
+178|
+179|  // 3. Concurrency check
+180|  if (typeof expectedRevision === 'number' && expectedRevision !== 
stateRow.revision) {
+181|    return res.status(409).json({
+182|      message: 'Game updated in another tab',
+183|      gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
+184|      archive: getArchiveForUser(userId)
+185|    });
+186|  }
+187|
+188|  // 4. Execute mutation in transaction
+189|  let responsePayload = null;
+190|  let statusCode = 200;
+191|
+192|  try {
+193|    const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+194|      const result = mutationFn(stateRow);
+195|      if (!result.valid) {
+196|        statusCode = 400;
+197|        responsePayload = {
+198|          message: result.error,
+199|          gameState: formatGameState(stateRow),
+200|          archive: getArchiveForUser(userId)
+201|        };
+202|        return;
+203|      }
+204|
+205|      const { newState, archiveMatch, unarchiveRoundId } = result;
+206|
+207|      // Update game state
+208|      db.prepare(`
+209|        UPDATE game_states
+210|        SET round_id = ?, board = ?, current_player = ?, status = ?, 
winning_cells = ?,
+211|            red_wins = ?, yellow_wins = ?, draws = ?, applied_history = ?, 
redo_history = ?, revision = ?
+212|        WHERE user_id = ?
+213|      `).run(
+214|        newState.round_id,
+215|        newState.board,
+216|        newState.current_player,
+217|        newState.status,
+218|        newState.winning_cells,
+219|        newState.red_wins,
+220|        newState.yellow_wins,
+221|        newState.draws,
+222|        newState.applied_history,
+223|        newState.redo_history,
+224|        newState.revision,
+225|        userId
+226|      );
+227|
+228|      // Handle archive addition
+229|      if (archiveMatch) {
+230|        db.prepare(`
+231|          INSERT INTO completed_matches (
+232|            user_id, match_id, round_id, result, final_board, moves, 
winning_cells, completed_at
+233|          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+234|        `).run(
+235|          userId,
+236|          archiveMatch.match_id,
+237|          archiveMatch.round_id,
+238|          archiveMatch.result,
+239|          archiveMatch.final_board,
+240|          archiveMatch.moves,
+241|          archiveMatch.winning_cells,
+242|          archiveMatch.completed_at
+243|        );
+244|      }
+245|
+246|      // Handle archive removal (on undo of terminal state)
+247|      if (unarchiveRoundId) {
+248|        db.prepare('DELETE FROM completed_matches WHERE user_id = ? AND 
round_id = ?').run(userId, unarchiveRoundId);
+249|      }
+250|
+251|      const updatedStateRow = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM game_states WHERE 
user_id = ?').get(userId);
+252|      const updatedArchive = getArchiveForUser(userId);
+253|
+254|      responsePayload = {
+255|        gameState: formatGameState(updatedStateRow),
+256|        archive: updatedArchive
+257|      };
+258|
+259|      // Store in operations for idempotency
+260|      if (operationId) {
+261|        db.prepare(`
+262|          INSERT OR REPLACE INTO operations (user_id, op_id, status_code, 
response_json, created_at)
+263|          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+264|        `).run(userId, operationId, 200, JSON.stringify(responsePayload), 
new Date().toISOString());
+265|      }
+266|    });
+267|
+268|    tx();
+269|  } catch (err) {
+270|    console.error('Mutation transaction error:', err);
+271|    return res.status(500).json({ error: 'Internal server error' });
+272|  }
+273|
+274|  return res.status(statusCode).json(responsePayload);
+275|}
+276|
+277|// Move mutation
+278|app.post('/api/game/move', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+279|  const column = req.body.column;
+280|  handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
+281|    return gameEngine.applyMove(stateRow, column);
+282|  });
+283|});
+284|
+285|// Undo mutation
+286|app.post('/api/game/undo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+287|  handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
+288|    return gameEngine.applyUndo(stateRow);
+289|  });
+290|});
+291|
+292|// Redo mutation
+293|app.post('/api/game/redo', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+294|  handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
+295|    return gameEngine.applyRedo(stateRow);
+296|  });
+297|});
+298|
+299|// New game mutation
+300|app.post('/api/game/new', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
+301|  handleMutation(req, res, (stateRow) => {
+302|    return gameEngine.applyNewGame(stateRow);
+303|  });
+304|});
+305|
+306|// Fallback to index.html for SPA
+307|app.get('*', (req, res) => {
+308|  res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
+309|});
+310|
+311|// Start server
+312|if (require.main === module) {
+313|  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
+314|    console.log(`DropLine server listening on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
+315|    console.log(`SQLite path: ${getDbPath()}`);
+316|  });
+317|}
+318|
+319|module.exports = app;

Summary: Test health check endpoint with single chained command

Reasoning:
Okay, here's my interpretation of that thought process, tailored for an expert 
audience, and presented as if it were my own internal monologue:

**Thinking Through Command Sequencing**

Alright, I'm at a point where I need to link together a few commands. Hmm, 
what's the best way to do this? My first instinct is to consider how the 
execution should proceed. Is it sequential and dependent on success, or should 
they run regardless of the previous command's outcome?

That's when I start thinking about command chaining. `&&` is the classic "and" 
operator, right? It means the second command only runs *if* the first one 
completes successfully. That's perfect when you have a sequence of operations 
that build on each other, where the next step is meaningless or could cause 
problems if the previous one failed.

Then again, what if I need them to run regardless? Maybe I'm cleaning up files 
and want to ensure a certain directory is emptied *and* logs are archived, even 
if one step trips up. In that case, the semicolon `;` becomes my go-to choice. 
It just executes them in order, no matter the exit status.

So, the decision boils down to the specific logic I'm trying to achieve. I'll 
need to evaluate the individual commands and their dependencies to determine 
whether I need conditional execution with `&&` or a straightforward sequence 
with `;`. Gotta consider the edge cases, too – how to handle errors gracefully. 
It all depends on the context and the desired outcome.

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Fix fallback wildcard route for Express 5 compatibility

Reasoning:
**My Initial Thoughts on Express 5.x Route Handling**

Okay, so it looks like Express 5.x and the updated `path-to-regexp` library have
changed how wildcard routes are handled. I can't just use `app.get('*', ...)` 
anymore. It's forcing me to be more explicit, either with a named group like 
`app.get('(.*)', ...)` or using `app.use` instead. This is a subtle but 
important shift in the framework's behavior.

Alright, no problem, I can adapt. Let's get this handled in my `/app/server.js` 
file. The goal here is to get any route that isn't already handled to render my 
`index.html` file. That way, the front end can handle the actual routing via its
client side router. I'll utilize `app.use` to cover the remaining requests that 
fall through all prior route definitions. I'll make sure to serve the static 
content as it should be with `res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 
'index.html'));`. Easy enough, let's update `server.js` and be done with it.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "str_replace"
  path: "/app/server.js"
  old_str: 
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

  new_str: 
    // Fallback to index.html for SPA
    app.use((req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'public', 'index.html'));
    });

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create /app/public directory

Reasoning:
**Frontend Design and Implementation Plan**

Alright, let's break down this frontend design. My goal here is to create a 
robust, accessible, and user-friendly interface for this Connect Four game, 
adhering precisely to the specifications provided in the instruction files. 
First, I will establish the basic HTML structure, then progressively add 
functionality.

I'll start with the foundational HTML elements within `/app/public/index.html`. 
The focus is on semantic structure and accessibility. I will structure the 
layout into two main views: a sign-in view and the main game view that appears 
once authenticated. The structure will use `div` elements with relevant IDs and 
classes for styling and scripting, including semantic elements for 
accessibility.

**Sign-In Section:**

This section will feature a welcoming title and subtitle, a form for email and 
password input, and a sign-in button. I'll include error feedback and 
helper/demo accounts. I'll carefully consider keyboard accessibility for form 
fields and the button.

**Game Section:**

The main game view, initially hidden, will become visible upon successful 
authentication. This section will contain the following key components:

*   **Header/Navigation**: A title and user information that displays the 
signed-in user's name and email, and the server revision badge with its number. 
The sign-out button is also included here.
*   **Match Totals (Scoreboard)**: This will clearly display the running totals 
for Red wins, Yellow wins, and Draws.
*   **Turn/Status and Feedback Banner**: This element will visually communicate 
the current turn (Red's or Yellow's) or game result (win or draw), and provide 
feedback for invalid moves or game updates.
*   **Board Section**: This will be the heart of the game. I will create a grid 
structure, with seven visible control buttons named "Drop in column 1" through 
"Drop in column 7". There will be focus handling and keyboard navigation between
these columns. The active board will be a 6x7 grid (42 `gridcell`s) with ARIA 
labels describing the state of each cell and will include winning cell styling.
*   **Action Controls**: "New game", "Undo" and "Redo" buttons will facilitate 
game interaction, ensuring keyboard usability. These controls must have state 
management for the `isActionPending` flag.
*   **Sidebar Sections**:
    *   **Move History**: A chronological list of applied moves, indicating the 
move number, color, column, and row for the current round. The "Move History" 
section includes empty message with no moves.
    *   **Completed Match Archive**: A listing of archived matches, showing the 
results, move count, and date. I'll use a well-formatted list.

**Replay Modal/Dialog:**

A modal dialog will be created for match replay functionality. It will feature a
title, match details, and a close button. The modal includes a read-only replay 
board with 42 cells. Each cell state will be derived from replay steps. Step 
information and a slider (with previous/next buttons) will allow navigation 
through the replay steps and ensures keyboard usability.

**Keyboard Navigation for Column Controls**:

I will ensure that left/right arrow keys move focus between the column buttons, 
Home goes to column 1, End to column 7, and Enter/Space triggers a drop in the 
focused column.

**Game Actions**:

*   **Handle Drop**: When a column button is clicked or activated via keyboard, 
the `handleDrop()` function will be called. It involves sending a `POST` request
to `/api/game/move`, updating the local game state, and ensuring that the column
button retains focus, using the `isActionPending` flag to prevent multiple 
actions during a request.
*   **Undo, Redo, New Game**: Similar `POST` requests will be dispatched for 
these actions (`/api/game/undo`, `/api/game/redo`, `/api/game/new`), with 
comprehensive state management, including managing `isActionPending`, to make 
the interface responsive.

**Error Handling**:

I'll thoroughly handle all possible server responses:

*   **401 Unauthorized**: Redirect to the sign-in form after clearing the 
authentication token.
*   **409 Conflict**: Update local game state with the authoritative state 
received from the server, and display a "Game updated in another tab" message.
*   **400 Bad Request**: Display appropriate feedback based on the error 
received (e.g., "Column N is full").
*   **200 OK**: Update local game state and clear invalid-move feedback, if any.

**Status Display and Winning Treatment**:

The game status display will clearly indicate the current turn or the final game
result (win or draw). I'll ensure winning cells are visually distinct, possibly 
with an animated glow or highlight,

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create /app/public/styles.css

Reasoning:
**Crafting the CSS for an Immersive Board Game Experience**

Okay, time to get into the nitty-gritty of styling `/app/public/styles.css`. My 
goal is to create a robust and polished CSS that's not just functional, but also
visually engaging. First and foremost, responsiveness is key – this has to look 
great down to 375px without introducing any nasty horizontal scrollbars. 
Accessibility is another huge priority. I need to ensure high contrast for all 
elements, crystal-clear focus rings for keyboard navigation, and distinct visual
cues to differentiate the Red and Yellow game pieces. I'll also be mindful of 
`prefers-reduced-motion` to offer a respectful experience for users who prefer 
less animation.

Let's dive into the details of the visual theme. I'm envisioning a rich, deep 
blue or indigo board with realistically rendered circular cutouts. The Red 
pieces will use a vibrant palette like `#d62828` or `#e63946`, while the Yellow 
pieces will utilize variations like `#f77f00`, `#fcbf49`, or `#e9c46a` for 
visual interest. Winning cells need a standout treatment – a bright, pulsating 
golden outline or glow and a clear marker to signify victory are essential. 
Think glowing borders, a sparkle indicator, or even a distinct winning badge.

The column controls are also important. I’ll make sure there are seven buttons 
aligned above the columns, with accessible names like "Drop in column 1" through
"Drop in column 7". The buttons will have either visible text or clear arrow 
icons. Keyboard focus indicators are non-negotiable – I'll go with a 
high-contrast outline like `outline: 3px solid #3b82f6; outline-offset: 2px;` to
ensure every user knows where their focus is.

For the mobile view at 375px, the board needs to be a seamless grid with seven 
columns. I'll use 10px of container padding and try to utilize the ~350px 
available for the board. Each column will be approximately ~44px wide with a 4px
gap, resulting in a board width of about 340px. The grid cells will be 42px x 
42px. I'll play around with border radii, shadows, and the responsive layout 
until it's just right.

Finally, I'll be considerate of motion sensitivities by leveraging `@media 
(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` to disable all animations and transitions. 
This will ensure that all users can enjoy the game in a way that feels 
comfortable to them. Now, let's get down to actually writing 
`/app/public/styles.css`.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/styles.css"
  file_text:

Summary: Create /app/public/app.js with client logic

Reasoning:
**My Plan for `app.js`**

Okay, let's break down this `app.js` file. First, I need to define the essential
data I'll be tracking. This includes the `token` (stored securely), the 
`currentUser` object with their ID, email, and name, and the critical 
`gameState`. The `gameState` is going to be the source of truth, coming directly
from the server and containing everything from the current round and the board 
layout to whose turn it is, the game's status, winning cells, all moves made so 
far, and the current revision. I'll also be storing an `archive` with the match 
history and will need to implement an `isActionPending` flag to prevent 
simultaneous requests, and a `currentReplay` object and a `feedbackTimeout` to 
handle temporary feedback messages.

Next, I need to manage all the DOM elements and the initial setup. I need to 
make sure I have all the elements for the sign-in section, game section, 
scoreboard, turn and feedback indicators, the game board itself (with the 7 
column buttons), and buttons for actions like "New Game", "Undo", and "Redo". 
History and archive sections, and of course, a replay modal will also need to be
hooked up.

The authentication flow is critical, so I'll design the logic to handle the 
initial page load, checking for a stored token and using that to get the user 
data, or failing to the sign-in screen if needed. I'll need to handle the 
sign-in process, posting credentials to the server and storing the returned 
token, and also the sign-out process, clearing the token and redirecting 
appropriately.

I'm going to implement robust keyboard navigation on those column controls. 
Using event listeners, I'll ensure that users can move focus between columns 
with the left and right arrow keys, jump to the first or last columns with 
"Home" and "End," and trigger a drop by pressing "Enter" or the spacebar.

The "Drop Column" action itself needs careful handling. The first step will be 
to check `isActionPending`, and if the flag is set, ignore the request. Then I 
need to update the flag and store the active column. I'll send a POST request to
the `/api/game/move` endpoint, including the column, revision, and a unique 
operation ID. Responses are crucial: I'll need to handle potential 401s, 409s 
(concurrency conflicts, which mean the state changed on the server while the 
request was in flight), 400s (invalid move errors), and successful 200 
responses. I need to be careful to restore focus to the correct column after any
action, including errors, and always clear the `isActionPending` flag.

I'll similarly handle "Undo," "Redo," and "New Game" actions, making sure to 
avoid concurrent requests and handling server responses (401, 409, 400, and 200)
appropriately, and always update the appropriate state or restore the focus.

The game board rendering needs to take into account the 42 cells. I need to get 
the color of each cell from the `gameState.board`. I'll also need to consider 
`gameState.winningCells` to apply appropriate ARIA labels, and render the 
board's visual state with the correct classes for the board and the grid cells.

The status and turn display will need to respond to the `gameState.status` and 
`gameState.currentPlayer`, showing the correct text and appropriate classes to 
visually indicate the current state of the game.

The scoreboard rendering is straightforward, displaying the red wins, yellow 
wins, draws, and the current revision number from `gameState`.

History rendering will need to show all previous moves, and I need to make sure 
to handle the case of an empty history gracefully. Each move will get a number, 
player color, and coordinate information, and it all has to be accessible.

The completed match archive section requires the correct total count and a list 
of matches. Each match entry needs to show the result, move count, completion 
date, and a "Replay" button.

When a user clicks "Replay," the replay modal needs to activate. I'll store the 
`currentReplay`, set the `replayStep` to 0, update the modal title and subtitle,
set the slider properties, show the modal, and render the board for step 0. The 
replay board rendering requires reconstructing the board state at a specific 
step in the replay and applying any winning cell highlights. The replay needs to
update the step caption, button states, and slider values for each step.

Event handlers for the replay controls need to be implemented for the slider 
input and the previous/next buttons. The UI must react to clicks on the close 
button, close-x, and overlay clicks.

Arguments:
  command: "create"

    /**
     * DropLine - Connect Four Client Application
     */
    
    (function () {
      'use strict';
    
      // State
      let authToken = localStorage.getItem('dropline_token') || null;
      let currentUser = null;
      let gameState = null;
      let archiveData = { totalCount: 0, matches: [] };
      let isActionPending = false;
      let lastTriggerElement = null;
    
      // Replay State
      let currentReplay = null;
      let currentReplayStep = 0;
    
      // DOM Elements
      const signinSection = document.getElementById('signin-section');
      const signinForm = document.getElementById('signin-form');
      const signinEmailInput = document.getElementById('signin-email');
      const signinPasswordInput = document.getElementById('signin-password');
      const signinErrorAlert = document.getElementById('signin-error');
      const demoFillBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.demo-fill-btn');
    
      const gameSection = document.getElementById('game-section');
      const userDisplayName = document.getElementById('user-display-name');
      const userDisplayEmail = document.getElementById('user-display-email');
      const serverRevisionNumber = 
document.getElementById('server-revision-number');
      const signoutBtn = document.getElementById('signout-btn');
    
      const statRedWins = document.getElementById('stat-red-wins');
      const statYellowWins = document.getElementById('stat-yellow-wins');
      const statDraws = document.getElementById('stat-draws');
    
      const gameTurnStatus = document.getElementById('game-turn-status');
      const gameFeedbackBanner = 
document.getElementById('game-feedback-banner');
    
      const gameBoardGrid = document.getElementById('game-board-grid');
      const dropColBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.drop-btn');
    
      const newGameBtn = document.getElementById('new-game-btn');
      const undoBtn = document.getElementById('undo-btn');
      const redoBtn = document.getElementById('redo-btn');
    
      const historyList = document.getElementById('history-list');
      const historyStepCount = document.getElementById('history-step-count');
      const noHistoryMsg = document.getElementById('no-history-msg');
    
      const archiveItemsList = document.getElementById('archive-items-list');
      const archiveTotalCount = document.getElementById('archive-total-count');
      const noArchiveMsg = document.getElementById('no-archive-msg');
    
      // Replay Elements
      const replayModalOverlay = 
document.getElementById('replay-modal-overlay');
      const replayModalTitle = document.getElementById('replay-modal-title');
      const replaySubtitle = document.getElementById('replay-subtitle');
      const replayBoardGrid = document.getElementById('replay-board-grid');
      const replayStepCaption = document.getElementById('replay-step-caption');
      const replayRangeSlider = document.getElementById('replay-range-slider');
      const replayPrevBtn = document.getElementById('replay-prev-btn');
      const replayNextBtn = document.getElementById('replay-next-btn');
      const replayCloseXBtn = document.getElementById('replay-close-x-btn');
      const replayCloseBottomBtn = 
document.getElementById('replay-close-bottom-btn');
    
      // Utility to generate UUID for operationId
      function generateOpId() {
        if (window.crypto && window.crypto.randomUUID) {
          return window.crypto.randomUUID();
        }
        return 'op-' + Date.now() + '-' + 
Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 11);
      }
    
      // API Client Helper
      async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
        const headers = options.headers || {};
        if (authToken) {
          headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${authToken}`;
        }
        if (options.body && typeof options.body === 'object') {
          headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
          options.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
        }
    
        try {
          const res = await fetch(path, { credentials: 'same-origin', 
...options, headers });
          const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
          return { status: res.status, ok: res.ok, data };
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('API request error:', err);
          return { status: 0, ok: false, data: { error: 'Network error. Please 
try again.' } };
        }
      }
    
      // Authentication
      function handleUnauthorized() {
        authToken = null;
        localStorage.removeItem('dropline_token');
        currentUser = null;
        gameState = null;
        archiveData = { totalCount: 0, matches: [] };
    
        signinSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        gameSection.classList.add('hidden');
        closeReplay();
      }
    
      async function checkAuthOnLoad() {
        if (!authToken) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
    
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/auth/me');
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          currentUser = res.data.user;
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive;
          showGameView();
        } else {
          handleUnauthorized();
        }
      }
    
      async function handleSignIn(e) {
        if (e) e.preventDefault();
        signinErrorAlert.classList.add('hidden');
        signinErrorAlert.textContent = '';
    
        const email = signinEmailInput.value.trim();
        const password = signinPasswordInput.value;
    
        if (!email || !password) {
          signinErrorAlert.textContent = 'Please enter both email and 
password.';
          signinErrorAlert.classList.remove('hidden');
          return;
        }
    
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/auth/signin', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: { email, password }
        });
    
        if (res.ok && res.data && res.data.token) {
          authToken = res.data.token;
          localStorage.setItem('dropline_token', authToken);
          currentUser = res.data.user;
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive;
          signinForm.reset();
          showGameView();
        } else {
          signinErrorAlert.textContent = res.data.error || 'Invalid email or 
password.';
          signinErrorAlert.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
      }
    
      async function handleSignOut() {
        if (authToken) {
          await apiRequest('/api/auth/signout', { method: 'POST' });
        }
        handleUnauthorized();
      }
    
      function showGameView() {
        signinSection.classList.add('hidden');
        gameSection.classList.remove('hidden');
        renderAll();
      }
    
      // Render Functions
      function renderAll() {
        if (!gameState || !currentUser) return;
    
        // User details
        userDisplayName.textContent = currentUser.name || 'User';
        userDisplayEmail.textContent = currentUser.email || '';
        serverRevisionNumber.textContent = gameState.revision !== undefined ? 
gameState.revision : '0';
    
        // Scores
        statRedWins.textContent = gameState.redWins || 0;
        statYellowWins.textContent = gameState.yellowWins || 0;
        statDraws.textContent = gameState.draws || 0;
    
        // Status & Turn
        renderStatus();
    
        // Board
        renderBoard();
    
        // Toolbar buttons
        undoBtn.disabled = !gameState.appliedHistory || 
gameState.appliedHistory.length === 0;
        redoBtn.disabled = !gameState.redoHistory || 
gameState.redoHistory.length === 0;
    
        // History
        renderHistory();
    
        // Archive
        renderArchive();
      }
    
      function renderStatus() {
        gameTurnStatus.className = 'turn-indicator';
    
        if (gameState.status === 'Red wins') {
          gameTurnStatus.textContent = 'Red wins';
          gameTurnStatus.classList.add('turn-red');
        } else if (gameState.status === 'Yellow wins') {
          gameTurnStatus.textContent = 'Yellow wins';
          gameTurnStatus.classList.add('turn-yellow');
        } else if (gameState.status === 'Draw') {
          gameTurnStatus.textContent = 'Draw';
          gameTurnStatus.classList.add('turn-draw');
        } else {
          const player = gameState.currentPlayer || 'Red';
          gameTurnStatus.textContent = `${player}'s turn`;
          gameTurnStatus.classList.add(player === 'Red' ? 'turn-red' : 
'turn-yellow');
        }
      }
    
      function showFeedback(msg) {
        if (!msg) {
          clearFeedback();
          return;
        }
        gameFeedbackBanner.textContent = msg;
        gameFeedbackBanner.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
    
      function clearFeedback() {
        gameFeedbackBanner.textContent = '';
        gameFeedbackBanner.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    
      function renderBoard() {
        gameBoardGrid.innerHTML = '';
        const board = gameState.board || Array(42).fill('');
        const winningCells = gameState.winningCells || [];
    
        for (let row = 1; row <= 6; row++) {
          for (let col = 1; col <= 7; col++) {
            const index = (row - 1) * 7 + (col - 1);
            const color = board[index] || '';
            const isWinning = winningCells.includes(index);
    
            const cellEl = document.createElement('div');
            cellEl.className = 'grid-cell';
            cellEl.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
    
            let ariaLabel = `Row ${row}, Column ${col}, ${color ? color : 
'empty'}`;
            if (isWinning) {
              ariaLabel += ', winning';
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-winning');
            }
    
            if (color === 'Red') {
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-red');
            } else if (color === 'Yellow') {
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-yellow');
            }
    
            cellEl.setAttribute('aria-label', ariaLabel);
            gameBoardGrid.appendChild(cellEl);
          }
        }
      }
    
      function renderHistory() {
        historyList.innerHTML = '';
        const moves = gameState.appliedHistory || [];
        historyStepCount.textContent = `${moves.length} move${moves.length === 1
? '' : 's'}`;
    
        if (moves.length === 0) {
          noHistoryMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
          return;
        }
    
        noHistoryMsg.classList.add('hidden');
        moves.forEach((move, i) => {
          const item = document.createElement('li');
          item.className = 'history-item';
    
          const numSpan = document.createElement('span');
          numSpan.className = 'history-move-num';
          numSpan.textContent = `#${i + 1}`;
    
          const playerSpan = document.createElement('span');
          playerSpan.className = `history-move-player color-${move.color}`;
          playerSpan.textContent = move.color;
    
          const coordsSpan = document.createElement('span');
          coordsSpan.className = 'history-move-coords';
          coordsSpan.textContent = `Column ${move.column}, Row ${move.row}`;
    
          item.appendChild(numSpan);
          item.appendChild(playerSpan);
          item.appendChild(coordsSpan);
          historyList.appendChild(item);
        });
    
        // Auto scroll history to bottom
        const historyContainer = 
document.getElementById('history-list-container');
        if (historyContainer) {
          historyContainer.scrollTop = historyContainer.scrollHeight;
        }
      }
    
      function renderArchive() {
        archiveItemsList.innerHTML = '';
        const total = archiveData.totalCount || 0;
        archiveTotalCount.textContent = `${total} total`;
    
        const matches = archiveData.matches || [];
        if (matches.length === 0) {
          noArchiveMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
          return;
        }
    
        noArchiveMsg.classList.add('hidden');
        matches.forEach(m => {
          const card = document.createElement('div');
          card.className = 'archive-card';
    
          const meta = document.createElement('div');
          meta.className = 'archive-meta';
    
          const resSpan = document.createElement('span');
          resSpan.className = 'archive-result';
          if (m.result.startsWith('Red')) resSpan.classList.add('res-red');
          else if (m.result.startsWith('Yellow')) 
resSpan.classList.add('res-yellow');
          else resSpan.classList.add('res-draw');
          resSpan.textContent = m.result;
    
          const detailsSpan = document.createElement('span');
          detailsSpan.className = 'archive-details';
          let dateStr = '';
          try {
            const d = new Date(m.completedAt);
            dateStr = d.toLocaleDateString() + ' ' + d.toLocaleTimeString([], { 
hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
          } catch (e) {
            dateStr = m.completedAt || '';
          }
          detailsSpan.textContent = `${m.moveCount} moves • ${dateStr}`;
    
          meta.appendChild(resSpan);
          meta.appendChild(detailsSpan);
    
          const replayBtn = document.createElement('button');
          replayBtn.type = 'button';
          replayBtn.className = 'btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary 
replay-open-btn';
          replayBtn.textContent = 'Replay';
          replayBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', `Replay match ${m.matchId}: 
${m.result}, ${m.moveCount} moves`);
          replayBtn.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
            lastTriggerElement = ev.currentTarget;
            openReplay(m);
          });
    
          card.appendChild(meta);
          card.appendChild(replayBtn);
          archiveItemsList.appendChild(card);
        });
      }
    
      // Game Mutations
      async function performMove(col) {
        if (isActionPending) return;
        isActionPending = true;
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/game/move', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            column: col,
            expectedRevision: gameState.revision,
            operationId: opId
          }
        });
    
        isActionPending = false;
    
        if (res.status === 401) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.status === 409) {
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
          showFeedback('Game updated in another tab');
          restoreColumnFocus(col);
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.status === 400) {
          if (res.data.gameState) {
            gameState = res.data.gameState;
            archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
            renderAll();
          }
          showFeedback(res.data.message || `Column ${col} is full`);
          restoreColumnFocus(col);
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          clearFeedback();
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
          restoreColumnFocus(col);
        }
      }
    
      async function performUndo() {
        if (isActionPending) return;
        if (!gameState.appliedHistory || gameState.appliedHistory.length === 0) 
return;
        isActionPending = true;
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/game/undo', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            expectedRevision: gameState.revision,
            operationId: opId
          }
        });
    
        isActionPending = false;
    
        if (res.status === 401) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.status === 409) {
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
          showFeedback('Game updated in another tab');
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          clearFeedback();
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
        }
      }
    
      async function performRedo() {
        if (isActionPending) return;
        if (!gameState.redoHistory || gameState.redoHistory.length === 0) 
return;
        isActionPending = true;
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/game/redo', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            expectedRevision: gameState.revision,
            operationId: opId
          }
        });
    
        isActionPending = false;
    
        if (res.status === 401) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.status === 409) {
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
          showFeedback('Game updated in another tab');
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          clearFeedback();
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
        }
      }
    
      async function performNewGame() {
        if (isActionPending) return;
        isActionPending = true;
    
        const opId = generateOpId();
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/game/new', {
          method: 'POST',
          body: {
            expectedRevision: gameState.revision,
            operationId: opId
          }
        });
    
        isActionPending = false;
    
        if (res.status === 401) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.status === 409) {
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
          showFeedback('Game updated in another tab');
          return;
        }
    
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          clearFeedback();
          gameState = res.data.gameState;
          archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
          renderAll();
        }
      }
    
      function restoreColumnFocus(col) {
        const btn = document.getElementById(`drop-col-${col}`);
        if (btn) {
          btn.focus();
        }
      }
    
      // Keyboard navigation for column drop controls
      function setupColumnKeyboardNav() {
        dropColBtns.forEach(btn => {
          const col = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-col'), 10);
    
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            performMove(col);
          });
    
          btn.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
              e.preventDefault();
              if (col > 1) {
                const prevBtn = document.getElementById(`drop-col-${col - 1}`);
                if (prevBtn) prevBtn.focus();
              }
            } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
              e.preventDefault();
              if (col < 7) {
                const nextBtn = document.getElementById(`drop-col-${col + 1}`);
                if (nextBtn) nextBtn.focus();
              }
            } else if (e.key === 'Home') {
              e.preventDefault();
              const firstBtn = document.getElementById('drop-col-1');
              if (firstBtn) firstBtn.focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'End') {
              e.preventDefault();
              const lastBtn = document.getElementById('drop-col-7');
              if (lastBtn) lastBtn.focus();
            } else if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
              e.preventDefault();
              performMove(col);
            }
          });
        });
      }
    
      // Replay Modal Handlers
      function openReplay(match) {
        currentReplay = match;
        currentReplayStep = 0;
    
        replayModalTitle.textContent = 'Match Replay';
        replaySubtitle.textContent = `${match.result} • ${match.moveCount} 
moves`;
    
        replayRangeSlider.min = 0;
        replayRangeSlider.max = match.moves.length;
        replayRangeSlider.value = 0;
    
        renderReplayStep();
        replayModalOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
        replayRangeSlider.focus();
      }
    
      function closeReplay() {
        currentReplay = null;
        replayModalOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
        if (lastTriggerElement && typeof lastTriggerElement.focus === 
'function') {
          lastTriggerElement.focus();
        }
      }
    
      function setReplayStep(step) {
        if (!currentReplay) return;
        const max = currentReplay.moves.length;
        currentReplayStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, step));
        replayRangeSlider.value = currentReplayStep;
        renderReplayStep();
      }
    
      function renderReplayStep() {
        if (!currentReplay) return;
    
        const maxMoves = currentReplay.moves.length;
        const step = currentReplayStep;
    
        // Compute board state at this step
        const board = Array(42).fill('');
        for (let i = 0; i < step; i++) {
          const m = currentReplay.moves[i];
          board[m.index] = m.color;
        }
    
        const isFinal = (step === maxMoves);
        const winCells = (isFinal && currentReplay.winningCells) ? 
currentReplay.winningCells : [];
    
        // Render 42 grid cells
        replayBoardGrid.innerHTML = '';
        for (let row = 1; row <= 6; row++) {
          for (let col = 1; col <= 7; col++) {
            const index = (row - 1) * 7 + (col - 1);
            const color = board[index] || '';
            const isWinning = winCells.includes(index);
    
            const cellEl = document.createElement('div');
            cellEl.className = 'grid-cell';
            cellEl.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
    
            let ariaLabel = `Row ${row}, Column ${col}, ${color ? color : 
'empty'}`;
            if (isWinning) {
              ariaLabel += ', winning';
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-winning');
            }
    
            if (color === 'Red') {
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-red');
            } else if (color === 'Yellow') {
              cellEl.classList.add('cell-yellow');
            }
    
            cellEl.setAttribute('aria-label', ariaLabel);
            replayBoardGrid.appendChild(cellEl);
          }
        }
    
        // Step Caption
        if (step === 0) {
          replayStepCaption.textContent = `Step 0 of ${maxMoves}: Starting empty
board`;
        } else {
          const lastMove = currentReplay.moves[step - 1];
          let cap = `Move ${step} of ${maxMoves}: ${lastMove.color} in Col 
${lastMove.column}, Row ${lastMove.row}`;
          if (isFinal) {
            cap += ` (${currentReplay.result})`;
          }
          replayStepCaption.textContent = cap;
        }
    
        // Buttons
        replayPrevBtn.disabled = (step === 0);
        replayNextBtn.disabled = (step === maxMoves);
      }
    
      // Setup Event Listeners
      function setupEvents() {
        // Sign in form
        signinForm.addEventListener('submit', handleSignIn);
    
        // Demo Fill Buttons
        demoFillBtns.forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            signinEmailInput.value = btn.getAttribute('data-email');
            signinPasswordInput.value = btn.getAttribute('data-password');
            signinSubmit();
          });
        });
    
        function signinSubmit() {
          handleSignIn();
        }
    
        // Sign out button
        signoutBtn.addEventListener('click', handleSignOut);
    
        // Toolbar buttons
        newGameBtn.addEventListener('click', performNewGame);
        undoBtn.addEventListener('click', performUndo);
        redoBtn.addEventListener('click', performRedo);
    
        // Column Controls
        setupColumnKeyboardNav();
    
        // Replay Controls
        replayRangeSlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          setReplayStep(parseInt(e.target.value, 10));
        });
    
        replayPrevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (currentReplayStep > 0) setReplayStep(currentReplayStep - 1);
        });
    
        replayNextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (currentReplay && currentReplayStep < currentReplay.moves.length) {
            setReplayStep(currentReplayStep + 1);
          }
        });
    
        replayCloseXBtn.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
        replayCloseBottomBtn.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
    
        replayModalOverlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
          if (e.target === replayModalOverlay) {
            closeReplay();
          }
        });
    
        document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Escape' && 
!replayModalOverlay.classList.contains('hidden')) {
            closeReplay();
          }
        });
    
        // Sync on tab focus / visibility change
        window.addEventListener('focus', syncStateIfVisible);
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
          if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
            syncStateIfVisible();
          }
        });
      }
    
      async function syncStateIfVisible() {
        if (!authToken || isActionPending) return;
        const res = await apiRequest('/api/game');
        if (res.status === 401) {
          handleUnauthorized();
          return;
        }
        if (res.ok && res.data) {
          if (res.data.gameState && gameState && res.data.gameState.revision !==
gameState.revision) {
            gameState = res.data.gameState;
            archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
            renderAll();
          }
        }
      }
    
      // Init
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        setupEvents();
        checkAuthOnLoad();
      });
    
    })();

+77|  async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
+78|    const headers = options.headers || {};
+79|    if (authToken) {
+80|      headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${authToken}`;
+81|    }
+82|    if (options.body && typeof options.body === 'object') {
+83|      headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
+84|      options.body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
+85|    }
+86|
+87|    try {
+88|      const res = await fetch(path, { credentials: 'same-origin', 
...options, headers });
+89|      const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
+90|      return { status: res.status, ok: res.ok, data };
+91|    } catch (err) {
+92|      console.error('API request error:', err);
+93|      return { status: 0, ok: false, data: { error: 'Network error. Please 
try again.' } };
+94|    }
+95|  }

+557|          e.preventDefault();
+558|          const firstBtn = document.getElementById('drop-col-1');
+559|          if (firstBtn) firstBtn.focus();
+560|        } else if (e.key === 'End') {
+561|          e.preventDefault();
+562|          const lastBtn = document.getElementById('drop-col-7');
+563|          if (lastBtn) lastBtn.focus();
+564|        } else if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') {
+565|          e.preventDefault();
+566|          performMove(col);
+567|        }
+568|      });
+569|    });
+570|  }
+571|
+572|  // Replay Modal Handlers
+573|  function openReplay(match) {
+574|    currentReplay = match;
+575|    currentReplayStep = 0;
+576|
+577|    replayModalTitle.textContent = 'Match Replay';
+578|    replaySubtitle.textContent = `${match.result} • ${match.moveCount} 
moves`;
+579|
+580|    replayRangeSlider.min = 0;
+581|    replayRangeSlider.max = match.moves.length;
+582|    replayRangeSlider.value = 0;
+583|
+584|    renderReplayStep();
+585|    replayModalOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
+586|    replayRangeSlider.focus();
+587|  }
+588|
+589|  function closeReplay() {
+590|    currentReplay = null;
+591|    replayModalOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
+592|    if (lastTriggerElement && typeof lastTriggerElement.focus === 
'function') {
+593|      lastTriggerElement.focus();
+594|    }
+595|  }
+596|
+597|  function setReplayStep(step) {
+598|    if (!currentReplay) return;
+599|    const max = currentReplay.moves.length;
+600|    currentReplayStep = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, step));
+601|    replayRangeSlider.value = currentReplayStep;
+602|    renderReplayStep();
+603|  }
+604|
+605|  function renderReplayStep() {
+606|    if (!currentReplay) return;
+607|
+608|    const maxMoves = currentReplay.moves.length;
+609|    const step = currentReplayStep;
+610|
+611|    // Compute board state at this step
+612|    const board = Array(42).fill('');
+613|    for (let i = 0; i < step; i++) {
+614|      const m = currentReplay.moves[i];
+615|      board[m.index] = m.color;
+616|    }
+617|
+618|    const isFinal = (step === maxMoves);
+619|    const winCells = (isFinal && currentReplay.winningCells) ? 
currentReplay.winningCells : [];
+620|
+621|    // Render 42 grid cells
+622|    replayBoardGrid.innerHTML = '';
+623|    for (let row = 1; row <= 6; row++) {
+624|      for (let col = 1; col <= 7; col++) {
+625|        const index = (row - 1) * 7 + (col - 1);
+626|        const color = board[index] || '';
+627|        const isWinning = winCells.includes(index);
+628|
+629|        const cellEl = document.createElement('div');
+630|        cellEl.className = 'grid-cell';
+631|        cellEl.setAttribute('role', 'gridcell');
+632|
+633|        let ariaLabel = `Row ${row}, Column ${col}, ${color ? color : 
'empty'}`;
+634|        if (isWinning) {
+635|          ariaLabel += ', winning';
+636|          cellEl.classList.add('cell-winning');
+637|        }
+638|
+639|        if (color === 'Red') {
+640|          cellEl.classList.add('cell-red');
+641|        } else if (color === 'Yellow') {
+642|          cellEl.classList.add('cell-yellow');
+643|        }
+644|
+645|        cellEl.setAttribute('aria-label', ariaLabel);
+646|        replayBoardGrid.appendChild(cellEl);
+647|      }
+648|    }
+649|
+650|    // Step Caption
+651|    if (step === 0) {
+652|      replayStepCaption.textContent = `Step 0 of ${maxMoves}: Starting 
empty board`;
+653|    } else {
+654|      const lastMove = currentReplay.moves[step - 1];
+655|      let cap = `Move ${step} of ${maxMoves}: ${lastMove.color} in Col 
${lastMove.column}, Row ${lastMove.row}`;
+656|      if (isFinal) {
+657|        cap += ` (${currentReplay.result})`;
+658|      }
+659|      replayStepCaption.textContent = cap;
+660|    }
+661|
+662|    // Buttons
+663|    replayPrevBtn.disabled = (step === 0);
+664|    replayNextBtn.disabled = (step === maxMoves);
+665|  }
+666|
+667|  // Setup Event Listeners
+668|  function setupEvents() {
+669|    // Sign in form
+670|    signinForm.addEventListener('submit', handleSignIn);
+671|
+672|    // Demo Fill Buttons
+673|    demoFillBtns.forEach(btn => {
+674|      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+675|        signinEmailInput.value = btn.getAttribute('data-email');
+676|        signinPasswordInput.value = btn.getAttribute('data-password');
+677|        signinSubmit();
+678|      });
+679|    });
+680|
+681|    function signinSubmit() {
+682|      handleSignIn();
+683|    }
+684|
+685|    // Sign out button
+686|    signoutBtn.addEventListener('click', handleSignOut);
+687|
+688|    // Toolbar buttons
+689|    newGameBtn.addEventListener('click', performNewGame);
+690|    undoBtn.addEventListener('click', performUndo);
+691|    redoBtn.addEventListener('click', performRedo);
+692|
+693|    // Column Controls
+694|    setupColumnKeyboardNav();
+695|
+696|    // Replay Controls
+697|    replayRangeSlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
+698|      setReplayStep(parseInt(e.target.value, 10));
+699|    });
+700|
+701|    replayPrevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+702|      if (currentReplayStep > 0) setReplayStep(currentReplayStep - 1);
+703|    });
+704|
+705|    replayNextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+706|      if (currentReplay && currentReplayStep < currentReplay.moves.length) 
{
+707|        setReplayStep(currentReplayStep + 1);
+708|      }
+709|    });
+710|
+711|    replayCloseXBtn.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
+712|    replayCloseBottomBtn.addEventListener('click', closeReplay);
+713|
+714|    replayModalOverlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+715|      if (e.target === replayModalOverlay) {
+716|        closeReplay();
+717|      }
+718|    });
+719|
+720|    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
+721|      if (e.key === 'Escape' && 
!replayModalOverlay.classList.contains('hidden')) {
+722|        closeReplay();
+723|      }
+724|    });
+725|
+726|    // Sync on tab focus / visibility change
+727|    window.addEventListener('focus', syncStateIfVisible);
+728|    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
+729|      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
+730|        syncStateIfVisible();
+731|      }
+732|    });
+733|  }
+734|
+735|  async function syncStateIfVisible() {
+736|    if (!authToken || isActionPending) return;
+737|    const res = await apiRequest('/api/game');
+738|    if (res.status === 401) {
+739|      handleUnauthorized();
+740|      return;
+741|    }
+742|    if (res.ok && res.data) {
+743|      if (res.data.gameState && gameState && res.data.gameState.revision 
!== gameState.revision) {
+744|        gameState = res.data.gameState;
+745|        archiveData = res.data.archive || archiveData;
+746|        renderAll();
+747|      }
+748|    }
+749|  }
+750|
+751|  // Init
+752|  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
+753|    setupEvents();
+754|    checkAuthOnLoad();
+755|  });
+756|
+757|})();

Okay, time to make sure this application is ship-shape and Bristol fashion, 
starting with the manifest. I need to document the essentials in 
`APP_MANIFEST.md`, and it needs to be right beside `server.js`. I have the 
checklist: the start command, the SQLite database path, and the API methods and 
paths. Got to be crystal clear on the sign-in, sign-out, reading game state, and
each mutation. Shared routes are fine; no need to be redundant. The database 
path gets written as `SQLite path: /app/dropline.db` in the manifest. Crucially,
normal restarts have to preserve the saved game state. And the manifest *must* 
also acknowledge the `DB_PATH` environment variable if it's set by the runner, 
otherwise, the default `/app/dropline.db` is the path to use. The runner, 
importantly, can launch a writable copy of the app as an unprivileged user, so 
all the permissions are handled properly. Time to get `file_editor` and craft 
that manifest!

  file_text: 
    # DropLine Application Manifest
    
    ## Start Command
    `node /app/server.js`
    
    ## Database
    SQLite path: /app/dropline.db
    
    ## Endpoints
    
    ### Health Check
    - `GET /api/health` - Server health status
    
    ### Authentication
    - `POST /api/auth/signin` - Sign in with email and password, returns Bearer 
token, user info, game state, and match archive
    - `POST /api/auth/signout` - Revokes all active bearer tokens for the 
authenticated account
    - `GET /api/auth/me` - Authenticates bearer token, returns user profile, 
current game state, and match archive
    
    ### Game State & Mutations
    - `GET /api/game` - Reads current game state and match archive for 
authenticated account
    - `POST /api/game/move` - Applies a column move (1-7), validates gravity and
win/draw, supports idempotency and concurrency checking
    - `POST /api/game/undo` - Undoes the most recent applied move, reverts score
on terminal moves, updates archive
    - `POST /api/game/redo` - Redoes the undone move, restores score and archive
on terminal moves
    - `POST /api/game/new` - Starts a new round, resets board while preserving 
match totals and archive
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

    const fs = require('fs');
    const path = require('path');
    const assert = require('assert');
    
    // Test DB path
    const testDbPath = '/tmp/test_suite_dropline.db';
    if (fs.existsSync(testDbPath)) fs.unlinkSync(testDbPath);
    process.env.DB_PATH = testDbPath;

    async function runTests() {
      console.log('--- Starting Comprehensive Test Suite ---');
      const server = app.listen(3002);
      const baseUrl = 'http://localhost:3002';
    
      try {
        // 1. Health check
        console.log('Test 1: Health check');
        let res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/health`);
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200);
        let json = await res.json();
        assert.strictEqual(json.status, 'ok');
    
        // 2. Static HTML check
        console.log('Test 2: Serve index.html');
        res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/`);
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200);
        let html = await res.text();
        assert.ok(html.includes('DropLine'));
        assert.ok(html.includes('Drop in column 1'));
        assert.ok(html.includes('Drop in column 7'));
    
        // 3. Unauthenticated request rejected
        console.log('Test 3: Unauthenticated /api/game rejected');
        res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/game`);
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 401);
    
        // 4. Invalid credentials rejected
        console.log('Test 4: Invalid signin');
        res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/signin`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 
'incorrect' })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 401);
    
        // 5. Sign in Avery Morgan (Seed state check)
        console.log('Test 5: Sign in Avery Morgan');

          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ email: 'avery@dropline.test', password: 
'password123' })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200);

        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.user.email, 'avery@dropline.test');
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.revision, 7);
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.redWins, 2);
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.yellowWins, 1);
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.draws, 1);
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.appliedHistory.length, 2);
        assert.strictEqual(averyAuth.gameState.redoHistory.length, 1);
        const averyToken = averyAuth.token;
    
        // 6. Avery Redo

        res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/game/redo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 
`Bearer ${averyToken}` },
          body: JSON.stringify({ expectedRevision: 7, operationId: 
'avery-op-redo' })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200);
        let stateData = await res.json();
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.revision, 8);
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.appliedHistory.length, 3);
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.redoHistory.length, 0);
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.currentPlayer, 'Yellow');

          method: 'POST',

`Bearer ${averyToken}` },

'avery-op-undo-1' })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(res.status, 200);

        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.revision, 9);
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.appliedHistory.length, 2);
        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.redoHistory.length, 1);
    
        res = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/game/undo`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': 
`Bearer ${averyToken}` },

'avery-op-undo-2' })

        stateData = await res.json();

        assert.strictEqual(stateData.gameState.redoHistory.length, 2);

        assert.strictEqual(conflictData.message, 'Game updated in another tab');
        assert.strictEqual(conflictData.gameState.revision, 4);

        console.log('Test 10: Valid move in Jordan account');

          method: 'POST',
